Computability - Chapter 215

1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the computability space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 106 and the convergence rate is O(192/n^2).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to computability theory, we decompose the problem into 106 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 192. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
